# One fixed confirmation and explicit lifetime provenance

The original V305 prose study remains fully preserved and timing-inconclusive. It completed all 32 cells and 32 warmups, passed the original work/output/build/physical-ceiling checks, and failed only the declared minimum eligible request/startup pair counts. Its observations are never pooled into a new acceptance result. The original campaign remains stopped, with all later paired stages and both legacy lifetimes unrun.

V320 is a distinct prospective campaign for the exact unchanged V304 binary, source archive and Metal library. Its qualification contract is byte-identical to V305 (dd1bada1296a7e69a034e7c674a92779b7d0f362dcd3dcfdb2cd2308baf8dbd0). Only the three qualified native stages and qualified short-one stage are copied byte-for-byte, with an explicit original-path/hash inventory. Their original recorded times and filesystem mtimes are retained; copied files naturally have new filesystem creation metadata. These are reused original executions, not newly measured passes. A fresh fixed prose confirmation plus the six original unrun paired workloads each get one attempt; all original 16 pairs, 32 cells, warmups, 180-second workspace quiet period, 60-second cooldowns, pools, sampling, retention, workload, exactness, physical ceilings and acceptance criteria remain. The original failed cohort is not retried or extended.

The V320 executor adds the same direct Foundation nominal/non-low-power check before a stage is claimed. It still uses the original memory-pressure, headroom, competing-work and owned-worker guards, a concrete later granted deadline, and only a persistent progress observer during timing. It cannot retry an attempted stage or launch a legacy lifetime after the paired matrix. Nine model-free test methods pass in 2.043169875 seconds, covering all thermal/power states, unavailable or malformed observations, expired/short/timezoneless reservations, altered imported and original proof, changed source/contract/helper identity, missing prerequisites, legacy lifetime attempts and no launch on refusal. No execution directory is created. Executor db5905eb381320d9845b794da2873324c3792941a9b2d844bf456b5e00cb8b43; preparation 398b0de56ffb6b49fb2b7f5860b68e8c666ecc0a46d15e9eceef655821f22a63.

V321 is the explicitly unrun successor to the preserved V312 lifetime executor. It binds the V320 provenance and byte-identical imported original proofs, retains the actual V311 public-planner receipt, and requires every native/paired gate to qualify before either lifetime. V308's exact two-field on-mode correction remains unchanged: on12GB/cap12GB, off10GB, the same six sequence positions, two warmup plus eight measured cycles (60 requests per mode), wire bodies, growth/exactness/resource criteria, deadlines and cleanup. Both old and new legacy lifetimes must stay unrun. Actual planner proof and 15 negative checks pass in 1.894330792 seconds. Neither mode is invoked. Executor 3114735434bce76c570c8654d80f3dfa4ec9e4df4f7e2c19283e6f73ba137680.

V322 verifies every one of the 150 frozen source-archive inputs and reconciles 48 Boolean optimization controls, five numeric controls, three construction choices and legacy environment source locations directly against the implementation. Every reference arm contains the complete 54-key explicit reference environment. The first four combined arms explicitly disable retention; the next three select the entire candidate including retention; the final combined arm is truly empty-environment default resolution. All original chunk256 choices match. The unchanged benchmark strips inherited SLOTSTREAM_ and SS_DEBUG variables before adding only frozen arm controls, detail output and the chunk, so legacy storage/scheduling overrides cannot leak from the shell. Ten Boolean defaults, checkpoint256 and row-backed embeddings are the intended qualified candidate, while experimental controls remain off and fused rotation retains its exact platform guard. Public ResidentWeights.arrays can materialize the original matrix, so this audit grants no unconditional resident-reserve credit.

This is preparation and source-level reconciliation, not a new runtime, performance, lifetime, empirical-calibration or activation pass. Shared defaults and release remain unchanged. The Mac is with the peer after the 17:54:55 UTC handback; no new model/compiler interval is granted and Chrome or other user apps are not closed. All seven confirmation workloads, both corrected lifetimes, four resource families, client extensions, fixed optional studies, calibration and activation remain required.

Original execution and exclusions: [[sources/runs/2026/09/2026-09-07-optimization-joined-eval-prose-qualification-exclusions]], [[sources/runs/2026/09/2026-09-07-optimization-joined-eval-short-request-pair]], [[sources/runs/2026/09/2026-09-07-optimization-joined-eval-current-gates]]. Actual consumer and lifetime correction: [[sources/runs/2026/09/2026-09-07-optimization-public-consumer-lifetime-preflight]], [[sources/runs/2026/09/2026-09-07-optimization-lifetime-mtp-vision-contract-correction]].

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/confirmed-lifetime-composition-v321/qualification.py

SHA-256 `3114735434bce76c570c8654d80f3dfa4ec9e4df4f7e2c19283e6f73ba137680`.

````text
"""Compose unchanged native/paired evidence with the corrected unrun lifetimes.

Only run-one launches a model, and it requires a fresh explicit granted deadline,
all qualified same-contract native/paired gates and the actual public-planner consumer proof.
The legacy campaign, its evidence, and the corrected V308 contract stay immutable.
"""
import argparse
import copy
import datetime
import fcntl
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import sys
import time

ROOT = Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream')
HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PRIOR = ROOT / '.build/optimization/thermal-screened-confirmation-v320'
CONFIRMATION = HERE.parent / 'thermal-screened-confirmation-v320'
CORRECTED = HERE.parent / 'lifetime-mode-contract-v308'
CONSUMER = HERE.parent / 'external-consumer-planner-v311'
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'Tools'))
import optimization_campaign as campaign
from prefill_bench import digest, preflight, vm_snapshot
from optimization_readiness import pressure_snapshot, require_normal
from optimization_serial_build import guarded_run


def require(value, message):
    if not value:
        raise ValueError(message)


def check_delta(original, successor):
    expected = copy.deepcopy(original)
    expected['soak_modes']['on']['memory_gb'] = 12
    expected['soak_modes']['on']['acceptance']['maximum_sampled_physical_peak_bytes'] = 12_000_000_000
    require(successor == expected, 'only the reviewed MTP/vision lifetime budget correction is permitted')


def confirmation_state():
    preparation = campaign.read(HERE / 'preparation.json')
    require(digest(CONFIRMATION / 'preparation.json') == preparation['confirmation_preparation_sha256'], 'confirmation provenance changed')
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('confirmation_v320', CONFIRMATION / 'confirmation.py')
    confirmation = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(confirmation)
    # This revalidates the stopped original cohort, all byte-identical imported
    # proofs, exact candidate/driver/template identities and both legacy soaks.
    return confirmation.status()


def load():
    confirmation_state()
    _, packet, original = campaign.load_packet(PRIOR)
    preparation = campaign.read(CORRECTED / 'preparation.json')
    require(digest(CORRECTED / 'predecessor-qualification-contract.json') == packet['contract_sha256'], 'predecessor differs')
    require(digest(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json') == preparation['new_contract_sha256'], 'successor changed')
    require(digest(CORRECTED / 'driver.py') == preparation['driver_sha256'], 'corrected driver changed')
    successor = campaign.read(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json')
    check_delta(original, successor)
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('corrected_lifetime', CORRECTED / 'driver.py')
    driver = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(driver)
    for mode in ('off', 'on'):
        driver.validate_protocol(successor['soak_modes'][mode])
    return packet, successor, driver


def planner_proof(build):
    protocol = campaign.read(CONSUMER / 'protocol.json')
    result = campaign.read(CONSUMER / 'manifest.json')
    receipt_path = CONSUMER / 'planner-lifetime-preflight.json'
    receipt = campaign.read(receipt_path)
    require(result.get('passed') is True and result.get('model_loaded') is False
            and result.get('source_unchanged') is True and result.get('protocol_sha256') == digest(CONSUMER / 'protocol.json'), 'actual public-planner consumer must pass')
    require(protocol.get('base_candidate') == build and protocol.get('source_sha256') == build['identity']['source'], 'consumer uses another build or source')
    require(protocol.get('consumer_script_sha256') == 'fddd0ed4018df6e0086b6915bbacc8622473321fa3e7b14dde55657d62060940', 'consumer preflight differs')
    destination = protocol.get('environment', {}).get('SLOTSTREAM_LIFETIME_PLAN_RECEIPT')
    require(type(destination) is str and Path(destination).resolve() == receipt_path.resolve(), 'receipt destination differs')
    require(receipt.get('model_loaded') is False and receipt.get('original_10gb_vision_refused') is True
            and receipt.get('existing_12gb_combined_plan_admissible') is True, 'actual combined-mode planner proof is missing')
    require(abs(receipt.get('combined_nominal_minimum_gb', 0) - 10.6) < 1e-8, 'combined floor differs')
    ledger = receipt.get('existing_12gb_combined_ledger', {})
    peak = ledger.get('expected_peak_bytes')
    require(type(peak) is int and 0 < peak <= 12_000_000_000, 'combined plan exceeds budget')
    capacity = receipt.get('existing_12gb_prefix_tokens')
    require(type(capacity) is int and capacity > 0, 'retained prefix capacity is missing')
    return campaign.evidence(CONSUMER, ['protocol.json', 'manifest.json', 'planner-lifetime-preflight.json', 'stdout.txt'])


def bound_protocol(mode, packet, contract, driver):
    protocol = campaign.prerequisite_protocol(PRIOR, contract, paired=True, mode=mode)
    protocol['qualification_contract'] = {
        'path': str(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json'),
        'sha256': digest(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json')}
    driver.validate_protocol(protocol)
    driver.validate_prerequisites(protocol, packet['build'])
    return protocol


def assess_mode(mode, packet, contract, driver):
    target = HERE / mode
    if not target.exists():
        return {'stage': 'corrected-lifetime/' + mode, 'status': 'unrun'}
    try:
        attempt = campaign.read(target / 'attempt.json')
        receipt = campaign.read(target / 'receipt.json')
        require(attempt['contract_sha256'] == digest(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json')
                and attempt['driver_sha256'] == digest(CORRECTED / 'driver.py')
                and attempt['executor_sha256'] == digest(Path(__file__)) and attempt['mode'] == mode, 'execution identity differs')
        require(receipt.get('exit_code') == 0 and receipt.get('cleanup_complete') is True
                and receipt.get('within_reservation') is True and not receipt.get('error'), 'execution failed or did not drain')
        require(attempt['planner_proof'] == planner_proof(packet['build']), 'planner proof changed')
        protocol = bound_protocol(mode, packet, contract, driver)
        raw = target / 'evidence'
        require(receipt.get('artifact_sha256') == campaign.artifact_hashes(raw), 'raw lifetime evidence changed')
        require(campaign.read(target / 'execution-protocol.json') == protocol, 'executed protocol differs')
        manifest = campaign.read(raw / 'manifest.json')
        require(manifest.get('passed') is True and not manifest.get('error')
                and manifest.get('build') == packet['build'] and manifest.get('protocol') == protocol, 'lifetime manifest failed or differs')
        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (raw / 'results.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
        expected = {item['name']: item for item in protocol['sequence']}
        for row in rows:
            item = expected[row['name']]
            metrics = row.get('metrics', {})
            stats = metrics.get('stats', {})
            require(row.get('succeeded') is True and stats.get('runtimeError') is None
                    and stats.get('decodeTokens') == item['outputs'] and len(metrics.get('output_ids', [])) == item['outputs']
                    and type(stats.get('promptTokens')) is int and 0 < stats['promptTokens'] <= protocol['maximum_prompt_tokens']
                    and metrics.get('effective_mtp') is (mode == 'on') and metrics.get('effective_prefill_chunk') == 256, 'a warmup or measured request violates its fixed workload')
        assessment = driver.assess(protocol, rows)
        require(assessment.get('passed') is True and assessment == campaign.read(raw / 'assessment.json'), 'raw lifetime criteria fail')
        return {'stage': 'corrected-lifetime/' + mode, 'status': 'passed', 'requests': len(rows)}
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as error:
        return {'stage': 'corrected-lifetime/' + mode, 'status': 'failed-or-incomplete', 'error': str(error)}


def status():
    packet, contract, driver = load()
    old = confirmation_state()
    require(all(row['status'] == 'unrun' for row in old['stages'][11:]), 'legacy lifetime evidence must remain unrun')
    stages = old['stages'][:11] + [assess_mode(mode, packet, contract, driver) for mode in ('off', 'on')]
    return {'format': 'slotstream-lifetime-composition-v2', 'confirmation_preparation_sha256': digest(CONFIRMATION / 'preparation.json'),
            'preserved_inconclusive_directory': old['preserved_inconclusive_directory'], 'build_identity': contract['build_identity'],
            'predecessor_contract_sha256': packet['contract_sha256'],
            'corrected_contract_sha256': digest(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json'),
            'stages': stages, 'combined_matrix_complete': all(row['status'] == 'passed' for row in stages),
            'legacy_campaign_complete': False, 'optimization_program_complete': False, 'activation_performed': False}


def run_one(deadline_text):
    require(digest(Path(__file__)) == campaign.read(HERE / 'preparation.json')['executor_sha256'], 'executor changed after qualification preparation')
    deadline = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(deadline_text.replace('Z', '+00:00')).timestamp()
    require(deadline - time.time() >= 1230, 'original 20-minute lifetime allowance plus cleanup must fit the explicitly granted interval')
    with (HERE / '.run.lock').open('a') as lock, (PRIOR / '.run.lock').open('a') as old_lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        fcntl.flock(old_lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        current = status()
        require(all(row['status'] == 'passed' for row in current['stages'][:11]), 'all same-contract native and paired stages must qualify before a lifetime starts')
        pending = next((row for row in current['stages'][11:] if row['status'] != 'passed'), None)
        if pending is None:
            return current
        require(pending['status'] == 'unrun', 'an attempted lifetime cannot be retried in this composition')
        mode = pending['stage'].split('/')[1]
        packet, contract, driver = load()
        proof = planner_proof(packet['build'])
        protocol = bound_protocol(mode, packet, contract, driver)
        require(not campaign.bench.competing_jobs(), 'competing work; model not launched')
        require_normal(pressure_snapshot())
        before = preflight(protocol['memory_gb'] + 3)
        target = HERE / mode
        target.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
        attempt = {'mode': mode, 'deadline_utc': deadline_text, 'contract_sha256': digest(CORRECTED / 'qualification-contract.json'),
                   'driver_sha256': digest(CORRECTED / 'driver.py'), 'executor_sha256': digest(Path(__file__)),
                   'planner_proof': proof, 'before': before}
        campaign.write_new(target / 'attempt.json', attempt)
        campaign.write_new(target / 'execution-protocol.json', protocol)
        policy = dict(campaign.NATIVE_POLICY, startup_reclaimable_bytes=(protocol['memory_gb'] + 3) * 1_000_000_000,
                      maximum_owned_rss_bytes=protocol['memory_gb'] * 1_000_000_000,
                      maximum_build_seconds=min(960, int(deadline - time.time() - 30)))
        command = [sys.executable, str(CORRECTED / 'driver.py'), '--protocol', str(target / 'execution-protocol.json'), '--out', str(target / 'evidence')]
        campaign.write_new(target / 'ownership-protocol.json', {'command': command, 'policy': policy})
        receipt = {'cleanup_complete': False}
        saved = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_', 'SS_DEBUG'))}
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            for key in saved:
                os.environ.pop(key)
            def snapshot():
                result = vm_snapshot()
                require_normal(pressure_snapshot())
                return result
            with (target / 'stdout.txt').open('w') as stdout, (target / 'stderr.txt').open('w') as stderr:
                result = guarded_run(command, cwd=ROOT, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr,
                                     record_path=target / 'memory.json', snapshot=snapshot, policy=policy,
                                     classification='Original bounded lifetime with prospectively corrected MTP/vision budget; no replacement, benchmark rescore or activation')
            receipt['exit_code'] = result.returncode
        except BaseException as error:
            receipt['error'] = f'{type(error).__name__}: {error}'
        finally:
            for key in list(os.environ):
                if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_', 'SS_DEBUG')):
                    os.environ.pop(key)
            os.environ.update(saved)
            receipt['elapsed_seconds'] = time.monotonic() - start
            receipt['within_reservation'] = time.time() <= deadline
            try:
                receipt['after'] = preflight(0)
                receipt['remaining_jobs'] = campaign.bench.competing_jobs()
                require_normal(pressure_snapshot())
                load()
                receipt['artifact_sha256'] = campaign.artifact_hashes(target / 'evidence')
                receipt['cleanup_complete'] = not receipt['remaining_jobs']
            except BaseException as error:
                receipt['cleanup_error'] = str(error)
            campaign.write_new(target / 'receipt.json', receipt)
        return status()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='action', required=True)
    sub.add_parser('status')
    run = sub.add_parser('run-one')
    run.add_argument('--deadline', required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    def interrupted(number, frame):
        raise KeyboardInterrupt(f'lifetime interrupted by signal {number}')
    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, interrupted)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, interrupted)
    report = status() if args.action == 'status' else run_one(args.deadline)
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    return 0 if args.action == 'status' or all(row['status'] in ('passed', 'unrun') for row in report['stages']) else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

````

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/confirmed-lifetime-composition-v321/validate.py

SHA-256 `8338e95d3bf4fa21c53e16f6083b24903db6296ab81ce12fc998da93482ba23f`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import ast
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
from unittest import mock

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
source = HERE / 'qualification.py'
ast.parse(source.read_text())
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('lifetime_v321', source)
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
packet, contract, driver = m.load()
original = m.campaign.read(m.CORRECTED / 'predecessor-qualification-contract.json')
m.check_delta(original, contract)
state = m.status()
assert len(state['stages']) == 13
assert sum(row['status'] == 'passed' for row in state['stages']) == 4
assert all(row.get('imported_original_execution') is True for row in state['stages'][:4])
assert not state['combined_matrix_complete'] and not state['optimization_program_complete']
assert state['preserved_inconclusive_directory'].endswith('joined-state-eval-campaign-v305/paired/unique-prose')
proof = m.planner_proof(packet['build'])
assert proof['sha256']['planner-lifetime-preflight.json'] == '0834b012c1794d592e56648abd8aba5fa7813ed731fa3c6f9c722d80aa1f6411'
refusals = []
for label, path, value in [
    ('changed build', ['build_identity', 'binary_sha256'], '0' * 64),
    ('changed native command', ['native', 'combined-plain', 'command'], []),
    ('changed paired request', ['paired_protocols', 'short-one', 'max_tokens'], 2),
    ('changed paired cap', ['paired_protocols', 'mtp-resource', 'memory_gb'], 12),
    ('changed off memory', ['soak_modes', 'off', 'memory_gb'], 12),
    ('weakened growth', ['soak_modes', 'on', 'acceptance', 'maximum_active_growth_bytes_per_position'], 128 << 20),
    ('replacement cycle', ['soak_modes', 'on', 'acceptance', 'replacement_cycles'], 1),
    ('different on budget', ['soak_modes', 'on', 'memory_gb'], 13),
    ('different cap', ['soak_modes', 'on', 'acceptance', 'maximum_sampled_physical_peak_bytes'], 13_000_000_000),
    ('shortened lifetime', ['soak_modes', 'on', 'measured_cycles'], 1),
]:
    bad = copy.deepcopy(contract)
    node = bad
    for key in path[:-1]:
        node = node[key]
    node[path[-1]] = value
    try:
        m.check_delta(original, bad)
    except ValueError:
        refusals.append(label)
    else:
        raise AssertionError('accepted ' + label)

read = m.campaign.read
for label, path, change, operation in [
    ('changed confirmation provenance', HERE / 'preparation.json', lambda d: d.update(confirmation_preparation_sha256='0' * 64), m.confirmation_state),
    ('missing planner validation', m.CONSUMER / 'manifest.json', lambda d: d.update(passed=False), lambda: m.planner_proof(packet['build'])),
    ('wrong planner receipt', m.CONSUMER / 'planner-lifetime-preflight.json', lambda d: d.update(existing_12gb_combined_plan_admissible=False), lambda: m.planner_proof(packet['build'])),
]:
    changed = copy.deepcopy(read(path))
    change(changed)
    def altered(p):
        return changed if Path(p) == path else read(p)
    with mock.patch.object(m.campaign, 'read', side_effect=altered):
        try:
            operation()
        except ValueError:
            refusals.append(label)
        else:
            raise AssertionError('accepted ' + label)

with mock.patch.object(m, 'guarded_run', side_effect=AssertionError('model must not launch')) as launch:
    for label, deadline, phrase in [
        ('expired interval', '2000-01-01T00:00:00Z', 'allowance'),
        ('unqualified confirmation matrix', '2099-01-01T00:00:00Z', 'must qualify'),
    ]:
        try:
            m.run_one(deadline)
        except ValueError as error:
            assert phrase in str(error), str(error)
            refusals.append(label)
        else:
            raise AssertionError('accepted ' + label)
    assert not launch.called
assert not (HERE / 'off').exists() and not (HERE / 'on').exists()
print(json.dumps({'passed': True, 'classification': 'Model-free confirmation/lifetime composition, actual public-planner proof, and prelaunch refusal checks only',
                  'refusals': refusals, 'original_execution_imports': 4, 'paired_stages_still_unrun': 7,
                  'actual_planner_proof': proof, 'model_compiler_or_server_launched': False,
                  'lifetime_attempt_directories_created': False, 'state': state}, indent=2))

````

````text
"""Reconcile exact candidate source and all final benchmark controls; no model."""
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import tarfile

ROOT = Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream')
HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
BASE = HERE.parent
BUILD = ROOT / '.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-build-v304/candidate'
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'Tools'))
import optimization_campaign as campaign


def sha(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


_, packet, contract = campaign.load_packet(ROOT / '.build/optimization/thermal-screened-confirmation-v320')
identity = packet['build']['identity']
assert campaign.digest(BUILD / 'build-source.tar.gz') == identity['source_archive_sha256']
with tarfile.open(BUILD / 'build-source.tar.gz') as archive:
    files = {}
    for member in archive.getmembers():
        if member.isfile():
            assert member.name in identity['source'], member.name
            raw = archive.extractfile(member).read()
            assert sha(raw) == identity['source'][member.name], member.name
            assert member.name not in files
            files[member.name] = raw.decode()
assert set(files) == set(identity['source'])
source = files['Sources/Slotstream/Optimizations.swift']
public_defaults = dict(re.findall(r'public var (\w+) = (false|true|\d+)', source))
flags = re.findall(r'result\.(\w+) = try flag\("([^"]+)", fallback: result\.(\w+)\)', source)
assert len(flags) == 48 and len({item[0] for item in flags}) == len(flags)
assert all(prop == fallback and public_defaults[prop] == 'false' for prop, key, fallback in flags)
numeric = {
    'prefixCheckpointTokens': ('SLOTSTREAM_OPT_PREFIX_CHECKPOINT', 'checkpointKey', [0, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]),
    'visionAttentionPadding': ('SLOTSTREAM_OPT_VISION_PADDING', 'visionPaddingKey', [0, 80, 128]),
    'visionQueryTile': ('SLOTSTREAM_OPT_VISION_QUERY_TILE', 'visionTileKey', [0, 256]),
    'workspaceTokenTile': ('SLOTSTREAM_OPT_WORKSPACE_TILE', 'tileKey', [256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]),
    'readScopeTokens': ('SLOTSTREAM_OPT_READ_SCOPE', 'scopeKey', [0, 1024, 4096, 8192]),
}
assert set(public_defaults) == {prop for prop, key, fallback in flags} | set(numeric)
for prop, (key, variable, values) in numeric.items():
    position = source.index(f'let {variable} = "{key}"')
    block = source[position:source.index(f'result.{prop} = n', position)]
    assert f'recognized.insert({variable})' in block
    assert '[' + ', '.join(map(str, values)) + '].contains(n)' in block
candidate_block = source[source.index('package static var integrationCandidate'):source.index('package static func deploymentCandidate')]
candidate = dict(re.findall(r'result\.(\w+) = (true|false|\d+)', candidate_block))
assert len(candidate) == 11 and candidate.pop('prefixCheckpointTokens') == '256'
assert len(candidate) == 10 and set(candidate.values()) == {'true'}
assert 'try resolving(environment: env, defaults: deploymentCandidate())' in source
assert 'public init() {}' in source
assert 'result.fusedRoPE = result.fusedRoPE && platform.qualifiedPartialRotation' in source
assert 'let unknown = env.keys.filter { $0.hasPrefix("SLOTSTREAM_OPT_") && !recognized.contains($0) }.sorted()' in source
assert 'guard unknown.isEmpty else { throw ModelError' in source
properties = {prop: key for prop, key, fallback in flags} | {prop: value[0] for prop, value in numeric.items()}
reference = {key: ('0' if public_defaults[prop] == 'false' else public_defaults[prop]) for prop, key in properties.items()}
reference['SLOTSTREAM_EMBEDDING_ROWS'] = '0'
assert len(reference) == 54
deployed = dict(reference)
for prop in candidate:
    deployed[properties[prop]] = '1'
deployed.update(SLOTSTREAM_OPT_PREFIX_CHECKPOINT='256', SLOTSTREAM_EMBEDDING_ROWS='1')
without_retention = dict(deployed, SLOTSTREAM_OPT_PREFIX_CHECKPOINT='0', SLOTSTREAM_OPT_COMPLETE_PROMPT='0')
matrix = []
for i, name in enumerate(campaign.PAIRED):
    protocol = contract['paired_protocols'][name]
    assert protocol['arms']['reference']['env'] == reference, name
    expected = without_retention if i < 4 else deployed if i < 7 else {}
    assert protocol['arms']['combined']['env'] == expected, name
    assert all(arm['binary'] == packet['build']['binary'] and arm['chunk'] == 256 for arm in protocol['arms'].values())
    matrix.append({'name': name, 'reference_controls': 54, 'combined_controls': len(expected),
                   'combined_family': 'retention disabled explicitly' if i < 4 else 'complete deployed family explicitly selected' if i < 7 else 'actual empty-environment default resolution',
                   'exact_environment_match': True, 'chunk': 256})
bench = (ROOT / 'Tools/serve_bench.py').read_text()
assert "clean_env={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG'))}" in bench
assert "env=clean_env|arm['env']|{'SLOTSTREAM_BENCH_DETAILS':'1','SLOTSTREAM_PREFILL_CHUNK':str(arm['chunk'])}" in bench
weights = files['Sources/Slotstream/Weights.swift']
model = files['Sources/Slotstream/Model.swift']
assert 'environment["SLOTSTREAM_EMBEDDING_ROWS"] ?? "1"' in weights
assert 'guard setting == "0" || setting == "1"' in weights
assert 'let useRows = embeddingRowCache ?? (setting == "1")' in weights
assert 'public let arrays: [String: MLXArray]' in weights
assert 'packGDNProjections: packGDNProjections ?? optimizations.fusedGDNProjection' in model
assert 'if let path = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["SLOTSTREAM_EXPERT_LAYOUT"]' in model
assert 'SLOTSTREAM_EXPERT_LAYOUT' not in reference and all('SLOTSTREAM_EXPERT_LAYOUT' not in arm['env'] for p in contract['paired_protocols'].values() for arm in p['arms'].values())
old = json.loads((BASE / 'control-disposition-preparation-v212.json').read_text())
old_flags = {item['property']: item for item in old['boolean_controls']}
assert set(old_flags) == set(prop for prop, key, fallback in flags)
controls = []
for prop, key, fallback in flags:
    prior = old_flags[prop]
    assert prior['environment'] == key
    controls.append({'property': prop, 'environment': key, 'public_initializer': False,
                     'candidate_default': prop in candidate,
                     'current_disposition': 'combined candidate; activation still requires complete current qualification' if prop in candidate else prior['disposition'],
                     'source_line': source[:source.index(f'public var {prop} =')].count('\n') + 1})
legacy = {}
for path, text in files.items():
    if path.startswith('Sources/Slotstream/'):
        for match in re.finditer(r'(?:environment|env)\["((?:SLOTSTREAM_|SS_DEBUG)[A-Z_]+)"\]', text):
            key = match.group(1)
            if key.startswith('SLOTSTREAM_OPT_'):
                continue
            legacy.setdefault(key, []).append({'file': path, 'line': text[:match.start()].count('\n') + 1})
result = {'captured_at': datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),
          'classification': 'Exact source/control reconciliation only. Existing execution evidence stays separately scoped; this audit grants no runtime, performance, calibration or activation pass.',
          'passed': True, 'build_identity': {key: identity[key] for key in campaign.IDENTITY_KEYS},
          'verified_archive_inputs': len(files), 'optimization_source_sha256': sha(source.encode()),
          'shared_source_sha256': campaign.digest(ROOT / 'Sources/Slotstream/Optimizations.swift'),
          'boolean_control_count': 48, 'numeric_control_count': 5, 'explicit_reference_environment_keys': 54,
          'boolean_controls': controls,
          'numeric_controls': [{'property': prop, 'environment': spec[0], 'allowed_values': spec[2], 'public_initializer': int(public_defaults[prop]), 'candidate_default': 256 if prop == 'prefixCheckpointTokens' else int(public_defaults[prop])} for prop, spec in numeric.items()],
          'constructor_controls': [
              {'name': 'embedding rows', 'candidate_default': True, 'reference_override': 'SLOTSTREAM_EMBEDDING_ROWS=0', 'constraint': 'Public arrays can materialize the original matrix. No unconditional resident-reserve credit is justified.'},
              {'name': 'shared GDN projection backing', 'candidate_default': False, 'constraint': 'Only explicit fusedGDNProjection or package constructor override selects it; rejected serving candidate stays off.'},
              {'name': 'packed expert layout', 'candidate_default': False, 'constraint': 'Only explicit nonempty SLOTSTREAM_EXPERT_LAYOUT selects a verified artifact; absent in all final arms.'},
          ],
          'kernel_selection': 'Automatic fused rotation is limited by exact native ARM64/Mac17,9/Apple M5 Pro/25G83 identity; other identities retain shared RoPE and the original rotation kernel.',
          'paired_environment_matrix': matrix,
          'legacy_environment_source_locations': legacy,
          'environment_isolation': 'Every child starts without inherited SLOTSTREAM_ or SS_DEBUG variables, then receives only its frozen arm overrides, benchmark detail output and chunk256. Legacy scheduling/storage overrides therefore cannot leak from the shell.',
          'shared_defaults_changed': False, 'runtime_or_compiler_or_server_launched': False,
          'activation_performed': False}
with (HERE / 'audit.json').open('x') as out:
    json.dump(result, out, indent=2)
    out.write('\n')
print(json.dumps({key: result[key] for key in ('passed', 'verified_archive_inputs', 'boolean_control_count', 'numeric_control_count', 'explicit_reference_environment_keys', 'shared_defaults_changed', 'activation_performed')}, indent=2))
````

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/thermal-screened-confirmation-v320/confirmation.py

SHA-256 `db5905eb381320d9845b794da2873324c3792941a9b2d844bf456b5e00cb8b43`.

````text
"""One independent confirmation, retaining the original inconclusive campaign.

Preparation imports only already-qualified same-contract evidence byte-for-byte.
The seven remaining paired workloads get one fixed attempt each. No sample from
the inconclusive prose cohort enters confirmation acceptance. Legacy lifetimes
are never executed here; their separate corrected contract remains required.
"""
import argparse
import datetime
import fcntl
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import signal
import sys
import threading
import time

ROOT = Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream')
HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PRIOR = ROOT / '.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-campaign-v305'
PACKET = ROOT / '.build/optimization/thermal-screened-confirmation-v320'
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'Tools'))
import optimization_campaign as campaign
import thermal_readiness
from prefill_bench import digest, preflight, vm_snapshot
from optimization_readiness import pressure_snapshot, require_normal
from optimization_serial_build import guarded_run

IMPORTED = campaign.STAGES[:4]
REMAINING = campaign.STAGES[4:11]
FAILED = 'paired/unique-prose'
THERMAL = ROOT / 'Tools/thermal_readiness.py'


def require(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)


def now():
    return datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()


def inspect_inconclusive(packet, contract):
    """Recompute full execution and prove that only minimum-pair gates failed."""
    target = PRIOR / FAILED
    attempt = campaign.read(target / 'attempt.json')
    receipt = campaign.read(target / 'receipt.json')
    raw = target / 'evidence'
    manifest = campaign.read(raw / 'manifest.json')
    completion = campaign.read(raw / 'completion.json')
    protocol = contract['paired_protocols']['unique-prose']
    require(attempt.get('stage') == FAILED and attempt.get('contract_sha256') == packet['contract_sha256'], 'prior attempt identity differs')
    require(receipt.get('error') is None and receipt.get('cleanup_complete') is True
            and receipt.get('exit_code') in (0, 1), 'prior execution failed or did not drain')
    require(receipt.get('artifact_sha256') == campaign.artifact_hashes(raw), 'prior raw artifacts changed')
    require(manifest.get('protocol') == protocol
            and manifest.get('arm_builds') == {'reference': packet['build'], 'combined': packet['build']}, 'prior build or workload differs')
    require(completion.get('interrupted') is False and completion.get('stopped_early') is False
            and 'error' not in completion and completion.get('planned_cells') == 32
            and completion.get('recorded_cells') == 32, 'prior cohort was not fully executed')
    rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (raw / 'results.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
    require(len(rows) == 32 and {(r['round'], r['arm']) for r in rows} == {
        (number, arm) for number in range(1, 17) for arm in ('reference', 'combined')}, 'prior cells are missing or repeated')
    warmups = {(r['round'], r['arm']): campaign.read(raw / f"{r['round']}-{r['arm']}/warmup.json") for r in rows}
    campaign.soak.validate_paired_execution('unique-prose', protocol, rows, warmups)
    summary = campaign.bench.summaries(rows, 'reference', protocol.get('comparison_basis', 'fixed-pool'), protocol.get('required_equal_work'))
    accepted = campaign.bench.acceptance_results(summary, protocol['acceptance'])
    startup = campaign.bench.startup_acceptance_results(rows, 'reference', protocol['startup_acceptance'])
    require(accepted == completion.get('acceptance') and startup == completion.get('startup_acceptance'), 'prior recorded and recomputed acceptance differ')
    require(len(accepted) == len(startup) == 1, 'unexpected prior acceptance arms')
    for result in accepted + startup:
        checks = result.get('checks', {})
        require(result.get('passed') is False and checks.get('minimum_pairs') is False
                and all(value is True for key, value in checks.items() if key != 'minimum_pairs'), 'prior failure was not limited to minimum eligible pairs')
    return {'classification': 'Complete exact execution; insufficient eligible request/startup pairs. Preserved and never pooled with the confirmation.',
            'cells': len(rows), 'warmups': len(warmups), 'acceptance': accepted, 'startup_acceptance': startup}


def prepare():
    _, old_packet, contract = campaign.load_packet(PRIOR)
    inconclusive = inspect_inconclusive(old_packet, contract)
    for stage in IMPORTED:
        campaign.assess_stage(PRIOR, old_packet, contract, stage)
    require(all(not (PRIOR / stage).exists() for stage in campaign.STAGES[5:]), 'original later stages must remain unrun')
    require(not (HERE / 'preparation.json').exists() and not PACKET.exists(), 'confirmation already prepared')
    campaign.prepare(old_packet['build']['binary'], contract['paired_protocols']['short-one']['model'], PACKET)
    require((PACKET / 'qualification-contract.json').read_bytes() == (PRIOR / 'qualification-contract.json').read_bytes(), 'confirmation contract must be byte-identical')
    imported = {}
    for stage in IMPORTED:
        original = PRIOR / stage
        target = PACKET / stage
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        hashes = campaign.artifact_hashes(original)
        shutil.copytree(original, target, copy_function=shutil.copy2)
        require(campaign.artifact_hashes(target) == hashes, 'copied qualification proof differs')
        imported[stage] = {'original_directory': str(original), 'sha256': hashes,
                           'meaning': 'Original execution reused without changing bytes, timestamps or acceptance.'}
    preparation = {'format': 'slotstream-independent-confirmation-v1', 'prepared_at': now(),
        'classification': 'One prospective fixed confirmation after environmental ineligibility; no retries, replacement cells, pooling, threshold change, runtime change or activation.',
        'prior_packet': str(PRIOR), 'packet': str(PACKET),
        'prior_packet_sha256': digest(PRIOR / 'packet.json'), 'packet_sha256': digest(PACKET / 'packet.json'),
        'contract_sha256': old_packet['contract_sha256'], 'build': old_packet['build'],
        'executor_sha256': digest(Path(__file__)), 'thermal_helper_sha256': digest(THERMAL),
        'imported': imported, 'preserved_inconclusive_stage': FAILED,
        'preserved_inconclusive_sha256': campaign.artifact_hashes(PRIOR / FAILED),
        'inconclusive_assessment': inconclusive, 'remaining_stages': list(REMAINING),
        'minimum_reserved_seconds': {stage: 3330 if stage == FAILED else 3630 for stage in REMAINING},
        'legacy_lifetimes': 'Both original and confirmation legacy soak directories must remain unrun. The separately corrected lifetime composition is required.',
        'reservation': 'No interval is reserved by preparation. Run only during a subsequently granted interval.',
        'model_launched': False, 'activation_performed': False}
    campaign.write_new(HERE / 'preparation.json', preparation)
    result = status()
    campaign.write_new(HERE / 'prepared-status.json', result)
    return result


def load():
    prepared = campaign.read(HERE / 'preparation.json')
    require(prepared.get('format') == 'slotstream-independent-confirmation-v1'
            and prepared.get('prior_packet') == str(PRIOR) and prepared.get('packet') == str(PACKET)
            and prepared.get('remaining_stages') == list(REMAINING), 'confirmation scope differs')
    require(prepared.get('executor_sha256') == digest(Path(__file__))
            and prepared.get('thermal_helper_sha256') == digest(THERMAL), 'confirmation executor or thermal helper changed')
    require(prepared.get('prior_packet_sha256') == digest(PRIOR / 'packet.json')
            and prepared.get('packet_sha256') == digest(PACKET / 'packet.json'), 'campaign packet changed')
    _, old_packet, original = campaign.load_packet(PRIOR)
    _, packet, contract = campaign.load_packet(PACKET)
    require(prepared.get('contract_sha256') == old_packet['contract_sha256'] == packet['contract_sha256']
            and original == contract and prepared.get('build') == old_packet['build'] == packet['build'], 'same exact contract and build required')
    require(prepared.get('preserved_inconclusive_stage') == FAILED
            and prepared.get('preserved_inconclusive_sha256') == campaign.artifact_hashes(PRIOR / FAILED), 'preserved inconclusive evidence changed')
    require(inspect_inconclusive(old_packet, original) == prepared.get('inconclusive_assessment'), 'prior failure disposition changed')
    require(set(prepared.get('imported', {})) == set(IMPORTED), 'exactly the four qualified stages may be imported')
    for stage in IMPORTED:
        proof = prepared['imported'][stage]
        require(proof.get('original_directory') == str(PRIOR / stage)
                and proof.get('sha256') == campaign.artifact_hashes(PRIOR / stage) == campaign.artifact_hashes(PACKET / stage), 'imported proof or original provenance changed')
        campaign.assess_stage(PRIOR, old_packet, original, stage)
        campaign.assess_stage(PACKET, packet, contract, stage)
    require(all(not (PRIOR / stage).exists() for stage in campaign.STAGES[5:]), 'original campaign must remain stopped')
    require(all(not (PACKET / stage).exists() for stage in campaign.STAGES[11:]), 'legacy confirmation lifetimes must remain unrun')
    expected = {stage: 3330 if stage == FAILED else 3630 for stage in REMAINING}
    require(prepared.get('minimum_reserved_seconds') == expected, 'reserved stage allowance changed')
    return prepared, packet, contract


def status():
    prepared, _, _ = load()
    result = campaign.status(PACKET)
    for row in result['stages'][:4]:
        row['provenance'] = prepared['imported'][row['stage']]['original_directory']
        row['imported_original_execution'] = True
    result.update(format='slotstream-independent-confirmation-v1',
                  preserved_inconclusive_directory=str(PRIOR / FAILED),
                  paired_matrix_complete=all(row['status'] == 'passed' for row in result['stages'][:11]),
                  optimization_program_complete=False, activation_performed=False)
    return result


def select_stage(rows):
    require([row['stage'] for row in rows] == list(campaign.STAGES), 'stage inventory differs')
    require(all(row['status'] == 'passed' for row in rows[:4]), 'imported prerequisites must qualify')
    require(all(row['status'] == 'unrun' for row in rows[11:]), 'legacy lifetimes cannot execute here')
    pending = next((row for row in rows[4:11] if row['status'] != 'passed'), None)
    if pending is not None:
        require(pending['status'] == 'unrun', 'an attempted confirmation stage cannot be retried')
    return pending


def require_thermal():
    observation = thermal_readiness.observe()
    require(observation.get('ready') is True and observation.get('conditions') == {
        'thermalState': 'nominal', 'lowPowerModeEnabled': False}, 'non-nominal or unavailable operating conditions; no stage claimed')
    return observation


def allowance(deadline_text, minimum):
    value = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(deadline_text.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
    require(value.tzinfo is not None, 'reservation deadline must include its time zone')
    deadline = value.timestamp()
    require(deadline - time.time() >= minimum, 'fixed study and cleanup must fit the granted interval; no stage claimed')
    return deadline


def run_one(deadline_text):
    with (HERE / '.run.lock').open('a') as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        current = status()
        pending = select_stage(current['stages'])
        if pending is None:
            return current
        stage = pending['stage']
        prepared, packet, _ = load()
        deadline = allowance(deadline_text, prepared['minimum_reserved_seconds'][stage])
        require(not campaign.bench.competing_jobs(), 'competing work; no stage claimed')
        require_normal(pressure_snapshot())
        before = preflight(13)
        thermal = require_thermal()
        # Check elapsed preparation again before claiming any wrapper or cell.
        allowance(deadline_text, prepared['minimum_reserved_seconds'][stage])
        target = HERE / 'execution' / stage.split('/')[1]
        target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'Tools/optimization_campaign.py'), 'run-one', '--packet', str(PACKET)]
        policy = dict(campaign.NATIVE_POLICY, maximum_build_seconds=int(deadline - time.time() - 30))
        protocol = {'stage': stage, 'prepared_sha256': digest(HERE / 'preparation.json'),
                    'executor_sha256': digest(Path(__file__)), 'packet_sha256': digest(PACKET / 'packet.json'),
                    'contract_sha256': packet['contract_sha256'], 'deadline_utc': deadline_text,
                    'command': command, 'policy': policy, 'before': before, 'thermal_prelaunch': thermal,
                    'classification': prepared['classification'], 'started_at': now(),
                    'progress_observer': 'One persistent thread reads only newly published completed cell rows. No subprocess, rescoring or workspace activity during timed study.'}
        campaign.write_new(target / 'protocol.json', protocol)
        receipt = {'passed': False, 'protocol_sha256': digest(target / 'protocol.json')}
        stopped = threading.Event()
        def progress():
            seen = 0
            while not stopped.wait(10):
                try:
                    lines = (PACKET / stage / 'evidence/results.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()
                    rows = []
                    for line in lines:
                        try:
                            rows.append(json.loads(line))
                        except json.JSONDecodeError:
                            break
                    if len(rows) > seen:
                        print(json.dumps({'stage': stage, 'completed_cells': len(rows), 'fixed_total_cells': 32,
                                          'eligible_cells_so_far': sum(row.get('valid') is True for row in rows),
                                          'acceptance': 'not yet assessed'}), flush=True)
                        seen = len(rows)
                except OSError:
                    continue
        observer = threading.Thread(target=progress, daemon=True)
        saved = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_', 'SS_DEBUG'))}
        start = time.monotonic()
        def snapshot():
            value = vm_snapshot()
            require_normal(pressure_snapshot())
            return value
        try:
            for key in saved:
                os.environ.pop(key)
            observer.start()
            with (target / 'stdout.txt').open('w') as out, (target / 'stderr.txt').open('w') as err:
                result = guarded_run(command, cwd=ROOT, stdout=out, stderr=err,
                                     record_path=target / 'ownership-memory.json', snapshot=snapshot,
                                     policy=policy, classification=prepared['classification'])
            receipt['exit_code'] = result.returncode
            after = status()
            receipt['stage_result'] = next(row for row in after['stages'] if row['stage'] == stage)
            receipt['passed'] = result.returncode == 0 and receipt['stage_result']['status'] == 'passed'
        except BaseException as error:
            receipt['error'] = f'{type(error).__name__}: {error}'
        finally:
            stopped.set()
            if observer.is_alive():
                observer.join(timeout=1)
            for key in list(os.environ):
                if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_', 'SS_DEBUG')):
                    os.environ.pop(key)
            os.environ.update(saved)
            receipt['elapsed_seconds'] = time.monotonic() - start
            receipt['within_reservation'] = time.time() <= deadline
            try:
                receipt['after'] = preflight(0)
                receipt['remaining_jobs'] = campaign.bench.competing_jobs()
                require_normal(pressure_snapshot())
                load()
                receipt['cleanup_complete'] = not receipt['remaining_jobs']
            except BaseException as error:
                receipt['cleanup_error'] = f'{type(error).__name__}: {error}'
            receipt['passed'] = bool(receipt['passed'] and receipt.get('cleanup_complete')
                                     and not receipt.get('cleanup_error') and receipt['within_reservation'])
            campaign.write_new(target / 'receipt.json', receipt)
        return receipt


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest='action', required=True)
    commands.add_parser('prepare')
    commands.add_parser('status')
    run = commands.add_parser('run-one')
    run.add_argument('--deadline', required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    def interrupted(number, frame):
        raise KeyboardInterrupt(f'confirmation interrupted by signal {number}')
    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, interrupted)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, interrupted)
    result = prepare() if args.action == 'prepare' else status() if args.action == 'status' else run_one(args.deadline)
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)
    return 1 if args.action == 'run-one' and result.get('passed') is False else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
````

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/thermal-screened-confirmation-v320/validate.py

SHA-256 `ac09375b500110242f3bf5ebce1272b4e52f8102b4f435f8b43b8c32cf2b13ea`.

````text
"""Model-free failure-boundary checks for the fixed confirmation executor."""
import ast
import copy
import datetime
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest
from unittest import mock

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('confirmation_v320', HERE / 'confirmation.py')
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)


def inventory():
    return [{'stage': stage, 'status': 'passed' if i < 4 else 'unrun'} for i, stage in enumerate(m.campaign.STAGES)]


class ConfirmationBoundaries(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_only_next_unrun_paired_stage_is_selected(self):
        rows = inventory()
        for i in range(4, 11):
            self.assertEqual(m.select_stage(rows)['stage'], m.campaign.STAGES[i])
            rows[i]['status'] = 'passed'
        self.assertIsNone(m.select_stage(rows))

    def test_failed_attempt_never_retried(self):
        for status in ('incomplete_or_failed', 'failed', 'running', 'unknown'):
            rows = inventory()
            rows[4]['status'] = status
            with self.subTest(status=status), self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'cannot be retried'):
                m.select_stage(rows)

    def test_missing_prerequisite_and_legacy_lifetimes_refused(self):
        for i in (0, 1, 2, 3, 11, 12):
            rows = inventory()
            rows[i]['status'] = 'unrun' if i < 4 else 'passed'
            with self.subTest(index=i), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                m.select_stage(rows)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'inventory'):
            m.select_stage(inventory()[:-1])

    def test_reservation_boundary_and_time_zone(self):
        deadline = '2030-01-01T00:00:00+00:00'
        seconds = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(deadline).timestamp()
        for minimum in (3330, 3630):
            with mock.patch.object(m.time, 'time', return_value=seconds - minimum):
                self.assertEqual(m.allowance(deadline, minimum), seconds)
            for remaining in (minimum - 0.01, 0, -1):
                with self.subTest(minimum=minimum, remaining=remaining), mock.patch.object(m.time, 'time', return_value=seconds - remaining), self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'fit'):
                    m.allowance(deadline, minimum)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'time zone'):
            m.allowance('2030-01-01T00:00:00', 3330)

    def test_thermal_and_power_states_use_actual_observer_validation(self):
        original = m.thermal_readiness.observe
        for thermal in range(4):
            for power in (False, True):
                observed = original(lambda: (thermal, power))
                with self.subTest(thermal=thermal, power=power), mock.patch.object(m.thermal_readiness, 'observe', return_value=observed):
                    if thermal == 0 and not power:
                        self.assertTrue(m.require_thermal()['ready'])
                    else:
                        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'no stage claimed'):
                            m.require_thermal()
        for error in (RuntimeError('provider unavailable'), ValueError('unknown state')):
            with mock.patch.object(m.thermal_readiness, 'observe', side_effect=error), self.assertRaises(type(error)):
                m.require_thermal()
        with mock.patch.object(m.thermal_readiness, 'observe', return_value={'ready': True, 'conditions': {}}), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            m.require_thermal()

    def test_actual_predecessor_is_complete_but_only_minimum_pairs_fail(self):
        _, packet, contract = m.campaign.load_packet(m.PRIOR)
        result = m.inspect_inconclusive(packet, contract)
        self.assertEqual((result['cells'], result['warmups']), (32, 32))
        self.assertFalse(result['acceptance'][0]['checks']['minimum_pairs'])

    def test_predecessor_interruption_wrong_build_raw_change_and_other_failure_refused(self):
        _, packet, contract = m.campaign.load_packet(m.PRIOR)
        raw = m.PRIOR / m.FAILED / 'evidence'
        read = m.campaign.read
        cases = [
            ('receipt.json', lambda d: d.update(cleanup_complete=False)),
            ('receipt.json', lambda d: d.update(artifact_sha256={})),
            ('evidence/manifest.json', lambda d: d.update(arm_builds={})),
            ('evidence/completion.json', lambda d: d.update(interrupted=True)),
            ('evidence/completion.json', lambda d: d.update(recorded_cells=31)),
            ('evidence/completion.json', lambda d: d['acceptance'][0]['checks'].update(exact_outputs=False)),
        ]
        for suffix, change in cases:
            path = m.PRIOR / m.FAILED / suffix
            changed = copy.deepcopy(read(path))
            change(changed)
            def altered(p):
                return copy.deepcopy(changed) if Path(p) == path else read(p)
            with self.subTest(suffix=suffix, value=changed.get('interrupted')), mock.patch.object(m.campaign, 'read', side_effect=altered), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                m.inspect_inconclusive(packet, contract)

    def test_expired_or_non_nominal_run_cannot_launch_or_claim(self):
        prepared = {'minimum_reserved_seconds': {stage: 3330 if stage == m.FAILED else 3630 for stage in m.REMAINING}}
        with mock.patch.object(m, 'status', return_value={'stages': inventory()}), mock.patch.object(m, 'load', return_value=(prepared, {}, {})), mock.patch.object(m, 'guarded_run', side_effect=AssertionError('model must not launch')) as launch:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'fit'):
                m.run_one('2000-01-01T00:00:00Z')
            with mock.patch.object(m.campaign.bench, 'competing_jobs', return_value=[]), mock.patch.object(m, 'require_normal'), mock.patch.object(m, 'pressure_snapshot', return_value={}), mock.patch.object(m, 'preflight', return_value={}), mock.patch.object(m, 'require_thermal', side_effect=ValueError('non-nominal')):
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'non-nominal'):
                    m.run_one('2099-01-01T00:00:00Z')
            self.assertFalse(launch.called)
        self.assertFalse((HERE / 'execution').exists())

    @unittest.skipUnless((HERE / 'preparation.json').exists(), 'run after preparation to verify real imports')
    def test_real_imports_and_all_frozen_proof_boundaries(self):
        prepared, packet, contract = m.load()
        state = m.status()
        self.assertEqual(sum(r['status'] == 'passed' for r in state['stages']), 4)
        self.assertFalse(state['paired_matrix_complete'])
        read = m.campaign.read
        changes = [
            lambda d: d.update(executor_sha256='0' * 64),
            lambda d: d.update(thermal_helper_sha256='0' * 64),
            lambda d: d.update(contract_sha256='0' * 64),
            lambda d: d.update(packet_sha256='0' * 64),
            lambda d: d.update(prior_packet_sha256='0' * 64),
            lambda d: d.update(build={}),
            lambda d: d.update(preserved_inconclusive_sha256={}),
            lambda d: d.update(inconclusive_assessment={}),
            lambda d: d['imported'].pop(m.IMPORTED[0]),
            lambda d: d['imported'][m.IMPORTED[0]].update(sha256={}),
            lambda d: d['imported'][m.IMPORTED[0]].update(original_directory='/wrong/provenance'),
            lambda d: d.update(remaining_stages=[]),
            lambda d: d.update(minimum_reserved_seconds={}),
        ]
        for i, change in enumerate(changes):
            changed = copy.deepcopy(prepared)
            change(changed)
            def altered(path):
                return changed if Path(path) == HERE / 'preparation.json' else read(path)
            with self.subTest(change=i), mock.patch.object(m.campaign, 'read', side_effect=altered), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                m.load()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    ast.parse((HERE / 'confirmation.py').read_text())
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
````
